金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 9 月 21 日
上週重點訊息
◼ Fed 三年來首次升息，點陣圖顯示年底前再升一碼：Fed 於 9/16 一致決議升息 1 碼，將聯
邦基金利率目標區間提高至 3.75%～4.00%，為 2023 年 7 月以來首次升息。最新利率預測
中，18 名官員有 16 人預期年底前至少仍需再升息 1 碼。最新經濟預測顯示，Fed 上修今明
兩年實質 GDP 成長率並下修失業率預估值，但也預期通膨壓力將比原先估計更為頑強。主席
Warsh 並指出美國經濟、就業、內需與資本投資仍具韌性，金融環境難稱緊縮。

◼ AI 安全疑慮引發半導體股急跌，但科技產業內部仍呈現明顯分化： OpenAI、Anthropic 等
AI 業者高層呼籲放慢 AI 發展速度、強化安全防護，引發市場重新評估 AI 投資成長速度與資
本支出前景，拖累費城半導體指數 9/14 重挫約 5.9%，但軟體股反而上漲。現階段尚不足以
證明 AI 資本支出循環已反轉，資金亦未全面撤離科技產業，而是從估值較高、籌碼較擁擠的
AI 硬體轉向其他科技次產業。
◼ 日本央行決議升息 1 碼，符合市場預期：日本央行 9/18 以 7 比 2 決議將無擔保隔夜拆款利
率由 1.0%調升至 1.25%。此為該行 2024 年來第 6 次升息，符合市場預期，故升息後對風險
資產衝擊有限。BOJ 明言將「繼續升息並調整寬鬆程度」 ，並將審視調整的時點與步調。
市場展望與操作策略
◼ 科技股中期趨勢未見反轉，但短線波動料加劇：儘管 FOMC 9 月升息，點陣圖暗示今年可能
再升一碼，但其對經濟成長率與失業率預期仍朝正向發展，同時股市也止跌上揚，顯示投資

人認為企業獲利成長動能足以抵消利率上揚的負面影響，基本面較佳的科技股仍具資金承
接，惟宜留意股市短線仍可能因地緣政治與經濟數據表現而出現波動。
◼ 中天期美債及投資級債風險報酬相對均衡：Fed 重啟升息且點陣圖暗示年底前或再升息，短天
期公債殖利率直接受到政策利率上修影響 ； 天期過長的債券則持續面臨財政赤字 、 發債供給及
期限溢價上升壓力，利率敏感度偏高。中天期(5~10 年期)美國公債既可取得目前相對較高收
益，亦具適度存續期間，在未來通膨降溫時較有機會受惠於殖利率回落，風險報酬較為均衡。
投資級債亦具較高收益與信用防禦特性，有助平衡投資組合波動。

## Document 028: 20261015【投研報告】市場快訊_通膨升溫壓抑美股表現，近期聚焦財報結果

Date: 2026-10-15

通膨升溫壓抑美股表現，近期聚焦財報結果
2026 年 10 月 15 日
一、 市場動態
1. 科技股領跌，費半連 6 日收黑：美股主要指數 10 月 14 日全數下跌，道瓊工業指數下
跌 0.09%，收 51,621.48 點；S&P 500 指數下跌 1.11%，收 7,718.94 點，為 7 月 29
日以來最大單日跌幅；Nasdaq 綜合指數下跌 1.88%，收 26,692.87 點，為 7 月 23
日以來最大單日跌幅。費城半導體指數下跌 4.30%，收 11,684.38 點，連續第６個交
易日走低，為 2025 年 5 月以來最長連跌紀錄。

2. 類股非全面下挫，資金仍呈輪動現象：半導體及 AI 相關股票在 10/14 跌幅尤其明顯
，AMD 下跌 5.73%、Nvidia 下跌 3.16%、Broadcom 下跌 3.10%，AI 基礎建設相關
的 CoreWeave、Nebius、Oracle 及 Dell 跌幅更深，顯示市場對資本支出規模較大、
融資成本敏感度較高的 AI 相關企業風險偏好明顯下降。不過，S&P 500 的 11 大類
股中有 8 類下跌，資訊科技及通訊服務為主要拖累來源，但醫療保健、必需消費與能
源類股仍逆勢上漲。
3. 美國 10 年期公債殖利率盤中急升後回落：美國 10 年期公債殖利率 10/14 由 5.2%左
右快速升至約 5.33%，逼近近期高點；然而，隨後市場消化 Fed 官員較審慎的政策訊
號，殖利率自高檔回落，終場約收於 5.19%，較前一交易日 5.21%小幅下降約 2 個基
點。
二、 下跌原因分析
1. 美 9 月通膨高於預期 ，Fed 再升息風險重新升高 ：美國 10/14 公布 9 月 CPI 月增 0.6%

、年增率也由 8 月的 3.4%上揚至 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%、年增率由 8 月的 2.4%
升至 2.6%。其中汽油價格單月大漲 9.1%，貢獻整體 CPI 月增幅超過一半；住宅成本
亦月增 0.4%，顯示物價壓力仍具黏性。此數據公布後，市場重新提高 Fed 進一步升
息的預期 ，CME FedWatch 所反映的 10 月升息 1 碼機率一度由前一日約 22%提高至
37%。雖然截至 10/15 台北時間早上 7 點左右該機率回降至 29.3%，這也提醒投資人
Fed 的升息路徑仍存在不確定性。
2. 高利率同時壓縮科技股估值與提高 AI 融資成本 ：科技及半導體公司未來獲利成長占評
價的比重較高，因此對折現率變化相對敏感。當長天期美債殖利率維持 5%以上，市
場願意支付的本益比自然受到限制，即使企業獲利仍維持成長，股價也可能因評價倍
數下降而出現修正。另一方面，本輪 AI 投資需要大量資料中心、伺服器、電力與網路
基礎設施，高利率亦直接增加企業融資成本，反映信用市場對高度依賴外部資金的 AI

基礎建設公司要求更高風險溢酬。

策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
3. AI 投資焦點由「支出規模」轉向「投資報酬率」:近期市場對 AI 的疑慮逐漸由「需求
是否成長」 ，轉向「龐大的資本支出能否轉化為足夠營收、獲利與自由現金流」 。
Broadcom 執行長陳福陽近期亦指出 ，AI 未來發展的重要限制可能逐漸由晶片或記憶
體供應轉向資本本身。我們認為，這並不代表AI 資本支出週期即將結束，而是市場已
由第一階段的 「資本支出愈大、成長想像愈高」 ，逐步進入更重視資本效率與投資報酬
率的第二階段。未來財務結構、現金流與AI 商業化能力，將成為不同企業評價分化的
重要因素。
4. 中國記憶體競爭疑慮加重半導體短線壓力：中國長鑫存儲（CXMT）10/10 傳出記憶

體技術取得進展 ， 引發市場對未來DRAM 供給增加及價格競爭的疑慮 ， 記憶體相關類
股率先承壓，並進一步影響整體半導體市場情緒。惟 DRAM 與 GPU、客製化 ASIC、
先進晶圓製程及先進封裝的產業競爭與需求結構並不相同。我們認為，儘管記憶體競
爭升溫值得關注，但目前尚不足以延伸為整體 AI 半導體需求同步轉弱。
5. 高檔籌碼加速獲利了結：AI 與半導體仍是 2026 年美股表現最強勢的投資主題之一，
部分科技股今年累計漲幅已相當可觀。當通膨、殖利率及AI 投資報酬疑慮同時升高，
先前漲幅較大、投資人部位較集中的族群自然成為優先調節對象。因此，本波費半快
速修正除了總經與基本面疑慮外，亦包含籌碼降溫及風險部位收縮因素，使短期價格
波動幅度可能高於企業基本面實際變化。
三、市場看法
1. 類股輪動明顯，尚非全面看空美股：科技與 AI 相關股票 10 月 14 日明顯承壓，但醫
療保健、必需消費與能源類股仍逆勢上漲，道瓊工業指數僅下跌 0.09%。此外，S&P
500 成分股下跌家數與上漲家數之比約為 1.1：1；相較於指數下跌 1.11%，市場廣度

並未同步大幅惡化。這反映資金主要由高評價且前期漲幅較大的科技與AI 族群，轉向
現金流較穩定的防禦型產業。現階段市場特徵較接近類股輪動與風格切換，而非無差
別的全面風險撤離，亦尚未看到美股企業獲利或經濟基本面全面反轉的明確訊號。
2. 美股科技股財報前風險降載，Q3 仍存在重新定價空間：近期科技股修正，費城半導
體指數連續６個交易日下跌，也可能部分反映第三季財報季正式展開前，投資人在股
價先前漲幅較大、評價偏高及利率環境不確定下，提前降低風險曝險。真正決定後續
市場方向的關鍵 ， 仍在於實際企業獲利能否支持目前的高成長預期 。根據 Bloomberg
季度獲利成長追蹤資料，今年 Q1 與 Q2 財報季期間的 EPS 成長率經驗顯示，隨實際
財報陸續公布，企業獲利成長曾明顯優於財報季開始前的市場預期。儘管這並不保證
Q3 將複製前兩季的上修幅度，但目前市場因財報不確定性所反映的風險折價仍存在
被實際財報重新驗證的可能。若大型科技及半導體公司後續營收、獲利與AI 需求指引
實際公布結果優於預期，近期風險溢價可望部分收斂；反之，若獲利成長無法跟上目

前評價，市場整理時間則可能進一步延長。

3. 費半修正幅度大，但 AI 需求尚未出現全面反轉：ASML 在 10/14 所公布的最新第三
季財報反而顯示 AI 相關設備需求仍相當強勁，該公司表示 2027 與 2028 年規劃產能
可能仍無法完全滿足客戶需求，部分客戶亦已表達對 2029 及 2030 年設備需求的興
趣。儘管費城半導體指數近期市場情緒轉弱，但由 ASML 的財報表現來看，目前仍未
看到大型 AI 客戶全面取消晶片訂單、主要雲端服務商同步削減 AI 資本支出，或先進
製程需求快速降溫等結構性訊號。
4. 台積電營收顯示 AI 需求暢旺 ， 留意10/15 法說會內容：台積電 10/8 公布的第三季營

收達新台幣 1.49 兆元、年增約 50%，創歷史新高，並高於市場原先預估的 1.46 兆元
；9 月單月營收年增 54.6%。強勁的營收表現仍反映 AI 與先進製程需求具有支撐。目
前市場因此呈現「企業營收仍快速成長，但股票評價下降」的狀況。高殖利率環境下
，EPS 上升與本益比下降可以同時發生，後續股價方向將取決於企業獲利上修幅度是
否足以抵銷評價收縮。該公司將於 10/15 下午舉行第三季法人說明會。市場重點將集
中於第四季展望、AI 與 HPC 需求、先進製程、先進封裝及未來資本支出規劃。公司
對 AI 需求與產能投資的最新判斷，將成為判斷近期費半修正主要屬於評價調整，或產
業基本面是否開始出現變化的重要依據。
5. AI 與半導體進入基本面分化階段：我們認為，在高利率環境下，高度仰賴外部融資、
自由現金流尚未成熟的 AI 基礎建設業者 ， 受到的資金成本壓力明顯高於具穩定現金流
的大型科技與雲端服務商。同樣地，記憶體、GPU、ASIC、先進製程及先進封裝各有
不同供需結構。我們預期下一階段 AI 行情將更加重視訂單能見度、產品競爭力、毛利

率、自由現金流及 AI 營收實際兌現，產業內部強弱分化程度可能進一步提高。
6. Fed 路徑仍存不確定性，惟企業獲利可望提供股市支撐：美國 9 月 CPI 升溫使市場重
新評估 Fed 後續升息時點與幅度，美國利率政策仍存在不確定性，長天期美債殖利率
也可能維持高檔。不過，現階段企業獲利成長與AI 相關需求仍具韌性，若第三季財報

延續前兩季優於預期的趨勢，企業 EPS 若能上修有望部分抵銷高利率對本益比的壓縮
效果。換言之，後續市場表現未必只由 Fed 政策主導，企業實際獲利能否持續兌現，
將是支撐科技與半導體股中期走勢的重要力量。
四、投資建議
1. 短線聚焦美股財報與 Fed 決策，科技股中期趨勢料不致有變：近期市場同時面臨財報
前的觀望態勢、美債殖利率偏高及 AI 資本支出疑慮等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能
受到壓抑，後續宜關注 10 月美股財報對企業獲利與資本支出展望，以及 FOMC 會議
所釋出的政策訊號。中期而言，目前AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡

象，科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，但在利率維持高檔及市場評價偏高的環境下，後
續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出的實際變化。
2. 中天期債券風險報酬仍較均衡 ：美國 9 月 CPI 升溫使短端殖利率持續受到 Fed 政策路
徑影響，而 20～30 年超長端仍面臨財政赤字、國債供給與期限溢價偏高等結構性壓
力。相較之下，目前殖利率已位於歷史相對高檔，若後續核心通膨或能源價格改善，
中段殖利率具有回落條件，同時其存續期間風險又低於超長端，因此現階段以中天期
美國公債及投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

- 2026 年 CAPEX 上調至 US$60-64bn(原為 US$52-56bn，接近上緣)；70~80% 於先進製程，10% 特殊
製程、10~20% 先進封裝、測試光罩 
- 資本支出通常對應未來數年更高的成長機會，目前看未來三年 CAPEX 會比過去三年再更為強勁 
- 已提前與設備供應商規畫產能，目前不預期設備供應會成為擴產計畫的重大瓶頸  
- 宣布將在亞利桑那追加 US$100bn 投資(總額提高至 US$265bn)，新增數座 N2 及更先進製程邏輯晶
圓廠、先進封裝廠 
AI 
- 先前提出的 2024-29 年 AI 相關營收「中高 50% CAGR」展望，管理層表示需求已變得「更強、更
強、再更強」，因為展望持續在調高所以不公布數字，實際趨勢高於先前說法  
先進封裝 
- 目前主流方案仍為 CoWoS，同時持續開發其他替代技術以降低封裝成本 
- 對 Intel EMIB-T 等先進封裝競爭，管理層認為後段和前段是完全不同等級，競爭者取得封裝訂單不

2026 獲利亮眼且擁高現金殖利率

永豐觀點 
2026 年獲利逆勢上揚且淡季運價緩跌有撐，擁8%現金殖利率。觀察12 月巴拿馬
運河水位。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：(1) 美洲持續暢旺到明年春節，亞洲進入旺季，運價淡季相對有撐，(2) 
2027 年新增運力被碼頭作業能量不易擴充而抵銷，實質有效運力可能低於市場預
期，(3) 高現金殖利率的保護，下檔有撐，維持買進，目標價由 256 元(0.9X2026 
BVPS)上調到 278 元(0.85X2027BVPS)。

暢旺到農曆春節，觀察 2Q27 美國是否啟動升息循環影響貨量。

2026 年供過增長率最低的一年：Alphaliner 預估 2026 需求成長 2.5%， 供給成長
率 4.7%，2026 年船舶供給成長率是近幾年最低的，目前貨櫃船在手訂單1400 萬
TEU，未來三年面臨新船大量下水壓力，然而公司認為受到地緣政治影響船期不穩
定性高、碼頭使用率偏高易受突發事件導致塞港、2028 年美西港口工人合約到期
等均影響有效運力，即使紅海復航採逐班逐航線，影響也會被抵銷，因此2027 年
的名目供給能否 100%轉為實質運力有待考驗 。 目前全球運力因為碼頭作業能量受
限、氣候因素、繞道、降速等影響 11%運力。 
美國零售進口 2026 年預計成長 1%：根據美國零售協會預估，2026 年 1-7 月貨
櫃進口 14.97 百萬 TEU(+0.3%YoY) ， 9 月進口量達到高峰 2.31 百萬
TEU(+10%YoY)，10 月開始下滑，但仍保持年成長，2026 年預估進口 25.7 百萬
TEU(+1%YoY)。

## Document 120: 261013_citi_voltronic-buy-initiation

### Page 1

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
Recharging Growth: UPS Recovery and HVDC Potential; Initiate at Buy
CITI'S TAKE 
We initiate coverage on Voltronic with a Buy rating and NT$1,050 target 
price. Voltronic is a Taiwan-based UPS ODM, supplying to western power 
vendors including Schneider, Riello, Vertiv and Eaton. We view 2026E as the 
earnings trough, with improving online UPS mix and operating leverage 
driving 28% earnings rebound in 2027E. Structural outsourcing by western 
power vendors should support sustained share gains, while expansion into 
HVDC power racks offers a new stream for AIDC growth, supporting a 
potential re-rating. Our TP applies 25x 2027E EPS against earnings CAGR 
of 20% in 2026-2028E, broadly consistent with its L-T historical average.
Outsourcing and mix upgrade underpin the recovery — Western vendors’ 
increasing focus on integrated AIDC infrastructure strengthens the rationale for 
outsourcing select UPS platforms. Voltronic’s customization capabilities and 
flexible manufacturing position it to capture additional opportunities. A rising 
contribution from online and higher power UPS should support margins, driving EPS 
CAGR of 20% in 2026-2028E. 
Power racks could materially broaden its growth opportunity — Voltronic’s high 
voltage EV-charger experience and longstanding relationships with western power 
vendors support its entry into HVDC power solutions. It has progressed into 
customer sampling, with management indicating that HVDC power rack mass 
production could begin in 2H27E at the earliest. Successful commercialization could 
broaden its addressable market and support a valuation re-rating.
Earnings recovery and strong cash returns  — We view 2026E as the earnings 
trough, followed by a recovery in 2027-2028E. A richer online UPS mix and operating 
leverage should lift GPM from 27.1% in 2026E to 28.1% and 29.0% in 2027E and 
2028E, respectively. Its relatively asset-light business model, net cash position and 
high dividend payout should provide additional support for shareholder returns.
Initiate Buy with TP NT$1,050 on 25x 2027E EPS — Our target multiple is broadly 
consistent with Voltronic’s average forward P/E since 2013, supported by its 
competitive position in UPS outsourcing and durable customer relationships. 
Clearer evidence of power-rack commercialization could offer further re-rating.

### Page 4

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
4
Executive Summary
We initiate coverage of Voltronic with a Buy rating and target price of NT$1,050
(25x 2027E EPS), supported by structural UPS outsourcing, improving product mix
and emerging opportunities in AI datacenter power infrastructure. Voltronic is a
Taiwan-based ODM of uninterrupted power supplies (“UPS”) and power
conversion products for global brands. UPS system provides backup electricity and

protection against power disturbances, helping critical equipment maintain
operation during supply disruptions. UPS accounted for 63% of Voltronic’s 2025
group sales. Its pure-play ODM model, customization capabilities and
longstanding customer relationships underpin its competitive positioning.
Our positive stance on the shares is based on three major reasons:
1. We see expansion into HVDC power racks as a potentially significant new
growth opportunity. Its experience in high-voltage EV chargers provides

relevant engineering capabilities, while its established relationships with
western power vendors support access to customer development programs.
Voltronic has progressed into customer sampling, with management
indicating that HVDC power rack mass production could begin in 2H27E at the
earliest. Successful commercialization could broaden its addressable market
and support a valuation re-rating, although meaningful earnings contribution
remains subject to qualification, design wins and production schedules.

2. Structural UPS outsourcing should support sustained market share gains.
Western power vendors are allocating more resources to integrated AIDC
infrastructure and energy management, strengthening the rationale for
outsourcing selected products. Voltronic’s engineering expertise, flexible
manufacturing and ability to handle a broad range of customized products in
small batches make it well positioned to capture these opportunities.
3. The shift toward online and higher power UPS should improve revenue quality

and profitability. We expect online UPS to be the principal driver of the
earnings recovery, with online UPS sales growing 12% CAGR in 2026-2028E as
outsourcing opportunities and demand for higher power systems expand.
We view 2026E as the earnings trough, followed by a recovery in 2027-2028E. We
forecast EPS of NT$33.00/NT$42.30/NT$47.25 in 2026E/2027E/2028E, implying
earnings growth of 28% and 12% in 2027E and 2028E. A richer online UPS mix and

operating leverage should lift GPM from 27.1% in 2026E to 28.1% and 29.0% in
2027E and 2028E, respectively. Its relatively asset-light business model, net cash
position and high dividend payout should provide additional support for
shareholder returns.
Our target price applies 25x forward P/E to 2027E EPS, broadly consistent with
Voltronic’s long tern average forward P/E since 2013. We believe its competitive
position in UPS outsourcing, durable customer relationships and manufacturing

capabilities justify this multiple, with clearer evidence of power rack
commercialization offering potential for further re-rating.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)

8
Investment Thesis
Positive #1 – AIDC creates a new growth runway, with
power-rack expansion potentially lifting Voltronic into
broader power solutions
We see Voltronic’s expansion into power racks as a potentially significant extension
of its AIDC opportunity in the medium to long term, enabling the company to
capture the broader share of datacenter power spending. We believe Voltronic’s
existing high-voltage engineering capabilities, established relationships with

western power vendors and customer collaboration provide a credible foundation
for winning business in HVDC power rack.
Rapid growth in AI workloads and rising rack density increase the power capacity
required, alongside more demanding requirements for efficiency, reliability and
load management. Nvidia’s roadmap targets 800VDC infrastructure supporting
racks of 1MW and beyond, while its latest architecture includes power racks that
supply 800VDC to compute racks within existing AC-powered facilities. We believe

this creates scope for greater power-infrastructure, even as the architecture
consolidates conversion stages to improve efficiency.
EV-charger experience provides a relevant technical foundation for HVDC
development
Voltronic already offers DC fast chargers ranging 30-240kW, with modular designs
delivering constant power across a 300-100VDC range and charging controls
developed internally. These products demonstrate existing experience with high-

voltage conversion, protection, thermal management and modular power-system
design. We believe these capabilities should support its development of 800VDC
PSUs and reduce the engineering learning curve.
Established westerns customer relationships strengthen its route to market
Voltronic’s UPS ODM business provides existing engineering relationships and
familiarity with customers’ quality, customization and manufacturing
requirements. Its pure ODM business model also emphasizes avoiding competition

with customers. We believe this positioning makes it a suitable partner for western
vendors who are expanding their AI infrastructure offerings. Existing relationships
should help Voltronic secure development opportunities and understand product
roadmaps early, although each new platform still requires qualification.
Voltronic has already taken concrete steps toward this opportunity. The company
has publicly introduced an HVDC PSU for power racks, while its broader

development plan extends into solutions combining battery backup unit (BBUs).
battery management systems (BMS), and DCDC converters through collaboration
with power-system companies.
Management indicated discussions with two Tier 1 vendors with the intended
solution ultimately servicing the service providers. This creates a potential path
from supplying individual power products to participating in a larger, integrated
power-rack offering.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 9

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
9
Development has progressed into customer sampling, although specifications
continue to evolve with customers’ requirements. Management pointed out that
shipment of 800VDC products possible from 2H27E at the earliest.
We view its existing relationships with global power-solution vendors as a key
advantage, while its commitment to operating as a pure ODM without competing
against customers under its own brand supports this partnership model.

That said, customer qualification, confirmed design wins and mass-production
timing remain key milestones before we can confidently evaluate a material
contribution. Sales potential also depends on Voltronic’s scope of supply, which
determines content per rack and the share of system value it captures. Progress
toward commercial shipments could strengthen earnings visibility and support a
re-rating as Voltronic establishes its position in AIDC power infrastructure.

Positive #2 – UPS outsourcing provides a structural share-
gain opportunity

We see structural outsourcing as a durable growth driver for Voltronic, allowing its
UPS business to outgrow underlying industry demand. Rising investment in digital
infrastructure, commercial applications and industrial automation supports
continued demand for power protection, while established brands have incentives
to outsource selected hardware platforms.
Voltronic’s dedicated ODM model, engineering capabilities and manufacturing
efficiency position it to capture this opportunity. In our view, growth can come from

both an expanding market and the transfer of existing production from customers’
factories to qualified manufacturing partners.
UPS remains a structurally growing power-protection market
Global Market Insights estimates the worldwide UPS market at US$13bn in 2025,
rising to US$14.5bn in 2026E and US$26.8bn by 2035E, implying a 7.1% CAGR
over 2026-2035E. Although estimates vary by research provider and market
definition, the underlying drivers include increasing dependency on electronic

systems, growth in industrial automation and smart manufacturing, increasing
power outages and grad instability, rising costs of downtime and greater
requirements for reliable electricity.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 11

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
11
Datacenters provide an important source of incremental demand, while
commercial and industrial applications broaden the market beyond the AI
investment cycle. Datacenters expansion supports demand for online, double
conversion UPS systems, which continuously condition incoming power and
protect critical equipment against supply disturbance. Commercial applications –
including offices, retail networks and healthcare facilities – require protection for

servers, communications equipment and other essential systems. Industrial
demand comes from protecting sensitive control, automation equipment and
critical processes where power interruptions can disrupt production.
The competitive landscape is led by established international suppliers. Per Global
Market Insights 2025 analysis, Schneider Electric, Eaton, Vertiv are the three
leading suppliers, with the top 5 collectively controlling approximately 60-65% of

the global UPS market. Other peers include Huawei, KSTAR, Kehua, Delta and
Riello.
This concentration matters for the outsourcing thesis – securing additional projects
from a relatively small number of large brands can open substantial manufacturing
opportunities.

Western brands’ strategic priorities support further selective outsourcing

Per our industry checks, major UPS brands still manufacture a substantial portion
of their UPS products internally, while increasingly moving toward energy
management and committing resources to broader AIDC infrastructure. This could
result in small and medium UPS products to be gradually outsourced as this
transition develops. We view this as a multi-year opportunity, particularly where
established platforms can be transferred to qualified ODMs while customers retain

control over product specifications, branding and distribution.
We identify three economic reasons for this shift –
1.Outsourcing can improve manufacturing and capital efficiency: A specialist
ODM can aggregate demand across customers, consolidate component
purchases and spread manufacturing overhead across a larger production
base. For brands, this can reduce incremental factory investment and simplify
capacity planning.
2.Outsourcing can accelerate product development: access to ODMs’

established platforms, component knowledge and manufacturing processes
allows brands to adapt existing designs for additional markets and
applications. Engineering resources can then concentrate on product
differentiation.
3.Broader system offerings create competing demands on vendors’ resources.
Leading UPS brands emphasize on monitoring, operational management and
integrated solutions, strengthening the rationale for outsourcing selected
hardware while allocating more resources to system integration, and software.

Voltronic’s dedicated ODM model, engineering capabilities and manufacturing
efficiency should position it to capture further UPS outsourcing

Voltronic’s ODM focus is evident from its commitment to operate without having a
brand of its own to compete with its customers. This aligns its interests with
customers and differentiates it from suppliers that combine branded sales with
contract manufacturing. Long-standing relationships with leading vendors,
including Schneider, Riello, Vertiv and Eaton, provide a foundation for securing

additional projects. Its engineering capabilities enable it to meet diverse product
specifications, while flexible manufacturing supports the small production batches
and broad model range characteristic of the UPS industry.
Its track record in product qualification and certification also creates barriers, as
moving established programs to other suppliers requires additional validation,
audits and testing. We believe this combination of customer trust, execution and

cost competitiveness positions Voltronic to secure more outsourcing orders,
including higher-power online UPS, as western brands allocate greater resources
to AIDC infrastructure and integrated power solutions.

Positive #3 – Online and high-power UPS mix upgrade
should support structurally better growth
We expect Voltronic’s shift toward online and higher-power UPS to become an
increasingly important driver of revenue growth and profitability. Moving up the

product range increases power capacity and engineering content per system,
supporting higher ASPs and greater differentiation. We forecast online UPS sales to
rise at a 12% CAGR in 2026-2028E, ahead of offline UPS at 6%, with online UPS
gross profit growing faster at 16% as product mix improves.
Offline UPS remains a stable revenue base, yet offers more limited differentiation.
Offline UPS typically serves home offices, small businesses and basic power

applications. These products feature relatively simple designs and lower costs.
We view this segment as increasingly mature and commoditized, with lower
technical barriers exposing suppliers to greater pricing pressure, including
competition from Chinese manufacturers. Scale, procurement efficiency and cost
control hence are the key competitive factors, while opportunities for sustained
ASP and margin expansion are more constrained, in our view. Our model assumes

offline UPS gross margin to be at 24-26% in 2025-2028E.
Online UPS offers greater technical differentiation and stronger margin potential.
Its double-conversion architecture continuously converts AC power into DC and
back into AC, providing stable voltage and frequency, avoiding power disturbances
and enabling zero transfer time to battery operation.
It is particularly suited to complex, power-sensitive applications such as
datacenters, industrial automation and precision control systems, where even brief

interruptions or power quality disturbances can disrupt operations. The business
also exhibits high-mix, low-volume characteristics, particularly in customized
Schneider, 18%
Vertiv, 5%

### Page 15

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
15
commercial and industrial applications, where customers require different power
ratings, redundancy configuration and battery interfaces etc. Our model assumes
online UPS gross margin to be at 31-34% in 2025-2028E.
We believe this creates opportunities for an experienced ODM partner such as
Voltronic to differentiate through customization and reliable delivery, while sharing
development and procurement resources across customers and product platforms.

T ogether with demanding certification, customer qualification and reliability
testing, these capabilities raise entry barriers and switching costs, supporting
higher ASPs, strong customer retention and better margin potential as Voltronic
expands into higher power systems.
We project online UPS sales to increase 12% CAGR in 2026-2028E, lifting its
contribution to group sales from 37% in 2025 to c.44% in 2028E. The underlying
driver comes from a stronger outsourcing opportunity thanks to the AIDC boom.

Western vendors, including Schneider, Vertiv are expanding their focus on
integrated AIDC infrastructure, power distribution, and infrastructure
management. Meanwhile, rising power requirements within conventional AC-
based datacenters support demand for higher power UPS systems, prompting
vendors to expand UPS production capacity. We believe the combination of
stronger demand and competing resource requirements can strain available
engineering and manufacturing capabilities, increasing the appeal of qualified

ODM partners to supplement internal production. Voltronic’s customization
capabilities and flexible manufacturing position it to capture this opportunity.

### Page 17

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
17
Financial Analysis
Income statement – 2026E marks the earnings trough; online UPS drives
the recovery from 2027E
Voltronic delivered an 8.7% sales CAGR and 9.9% net profit CAGR over 2018-2025,
although earnings have become more volatile in recent years due largely to swings
in the inverter business. We forecast sales to decrease 1% y/y to NT$20.4bn in
2026E, before recovering 8% y/y and 7% y/y to NT$21.9bn and NT$23.4bn in

2027E and 2028E respectively. The key driver is the higher value online UPS.
We forecast online UPS sales to grow 10%/13%/10% y/y in 2026E/2027E/2028E,
lifting its sales contribution from 37% in 2025 to 44% by 2028E. T ogether with
offline UPS, we estimate UPS products to make up 72% of group sales in 2028E vs
2025’s 63%. Conversely, we forecast inverter sales to decline 27% y/y in 2026E and
remain broadly flat thereafter, reducing their sales contribution from 29% in 2025

to 18% in 2028E.
We expect gross margin to bottom at 27.1% in 2026E vs. 29.0% in 2025, reflecting
negative impact from material cost inflation and weaker inverter profitability.
Thereafter, we forecast gross margin to recover to 28.1% and 29.0% in 2027E and
2028E, supported by a richer online UPS mix and improving segment margins. We
estimate online UPS gross margin to increase from 30.8% in 2026E to 33.4% in
2028E driven rising contribution from high power online UPS, while its rising sales

mix could provide increasing contribution to group gross profits.
Operating leverage should further support profitability. Opex fell from 8.0% of sales
in 2025 to 7.4% in 2026E and could remain at 7.2% thereafter, while R&D
investment is maintained at 4.2% of sales. We therefore forecast OPM to recover
from 19.7% in 2026E to 20.9% and 21.8% in 2027E and 2028E.
We estimate net profit to decrease 18% y/y in 2026E, partly hampered by a larger
FX loss, before rebounding 28% y/y and 12% y/y in 2027E and 2028E. We forecast

EPS to register NT$33.00/NT$42.30/NT$47.25 in 2026E/2027E/2028E.
We attribute Voltronic’s earnings decline in 2025 and our forecast for a further
decline in 2026E to persistent inverter weakness and additional cost and supply
pressures. In 2025, adverse weather in Pakistan shortened the inverter peak
season and weakened demand, while intensifying Chinese competition pressured
pricing and profitability. The weakness extended into 2026E as the Middle East

conflict disrupted logistics and delayed shipments, alongside continued customers
destocking in Pakistan. In 1H26, RMB appreciation further weighed on margins.
Meanwhile, raw material shortages constrained shipments and higher metal and
key component prices eroded gross margins. We therefore forecast earnings to
decline 18% y/y in 2026E before improving 28% y/y in 2027E.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Balance Sheet – Asset light model and net cash suggest high returns
Voltronic maintains a relatively asset-light balance sheet with fixed assets making
up 24% of total assets in 2025 despite its sizable sales scale. We estimate the

company to remain in a net cash position throughout 2026-2028E. We forecast
cash and cash equivalent assets to account for 36-39% of total assets in 2026-
2028E while there are no meaningful short-term borrowings and long-term debt.
We forecast ROE to rise from 37% in 2025 to 40% in 2026E and 48% in 2027E. The
high ROE is not driven by financial leverage, but primarily by its asset-light
business model, strong operating margins and high capital distribution.

Voltronic also maintains high shareholder-return policy. We assume an 87% cash
dividend payout ratio through 2026-2028E, implying cash dividends of
NT$29/NT$37/NT$41 per share.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
We value Voltronic using a forward target P/E of 25x our 2027E EPS to derive a
target price of NT$1,050, broadly in line with its long-term average forward P/E
since 2013. We believe forward P/E is appropriate given the company’s established
earnings base and the importance of product mix and operating leverage to its
profitability. Using 2027E EPS captures a fuller year of anticipated earnings

recovery following the softer 2026E base, alongside the ongoing transition toward
online and higher power UPS. This also aligns our valuation with the earnings
outlook investors are likely to focus on as the recovery progresses.
Our target multiple of 25x is supported by Voltronic’s competitive position in UPS
outsourcing. Its established relationship with western power vendors and ability to
accommodate high mix, low volume production creates a solid foundation for the

intact share gains, supporting the durability of its earnings and hence justify a
valuation broadly consistent with its historical average. Stronger evidence of power
rack commercialization and sustained UPS mix improvement could support a
further re-rating.

23
Key downside risks to our Buy rating and target price include:
1.Slower UPS demand or outsourcing gain. Weaker commercial and industrial
spending, delayed datacenter projects or customer inventory adjustments
could weigh on orders.
2.  Less favorable product mix and margin pressure. A slower transition toward
online and high-power UPS, more aggressive pricing or higher component
costs could limit margin improvement.

3. Persistent PV inverter competition. Aggressive pricing by Chinese suppliers,
weaker residential solar demand or unfavorable subsidy changes could
pressure inverter sales and profitability.
Our quantitative model assigns a High Risk rating to Voltronic. However, we
believe such a rating is not warranted due to: 1) its established franchise and
longstanding relationships with leading power vendors, with demanding
qualification requirements and high customization supporting customer retention

and repeat business; 2) its track record of profitability and positive operating cash
flow enables internally funded investment and reduces reliance on external
financing; and 3) the company has a strong balance sheet and has been in net cash
position.

### Page 24

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)

24
Company Overview
Established in 2008 and listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 2014, Voltronic
Power is a Taiwan-based design and manufacturing service (DMS) provider
specializing in uninterrupted power supplies (UPS) and power-conversion
products. The company is headquartered in Taipei and operates manufacturing
facilities in Taiwan, China and Vietnam, with approximately 5,000 employees.

Voltronic adopts a pure-play ODM business model and does not market products
under its own brand. This avoids direct competition with customers and has helped
the company establish long-term relationships with most global Tier-1 UPS
brands, as well as numerous regional brand owners.
The company provides customers with product design, R&D, sourcing,
manufacturing and system integration services, with a focus on high-mix,
relatively low-volume and customized products.

Voltronic’s core products include offline UPS systems ranging from 400VA to 3kVA
and on-line UPS systems ranging from 1kVA to 3MVA. The company has
progressively expanded from conventional single phase UPS products into three-
phase, high-power and modular UPS systems. It has also diversified into off-grid
and hybrid solar inverters, energy storage systems, and EV chargers.
In 2025, online UPS contributed 37% of group sales, offline UPS 26%, inverters

29% and others 8%. By geo, Asia accounted for 60% of group sales, followed by
Americas 22%, EU 16% and others 2%.

Shareholder Structure
As of March 2026, founder, chairman and general manager Alex Hsieh was the

largest individual shareholder, holding 9.6% of outstanding shares. Based on
disclosed direct, family-related and investment company holdings, we estimate
the founder and related parties collectively controlled c.30% of outstanding
shares.
Key Management Team
nAlex Hsieh – Founder, Chairman and General Manager: Mr. Hsieh has led
Voltronic since its establishment in 2008. He holds a bachelor’s degree in
business management from Tatung University and previously served as

Chairman and General Manager of Centralion Industrial and as a director of
Phoenixtec Power. His extensive experience in the UPS industry underpins
Voltronic’s pure-play ODM strategy.
nMonica Wang – Vice General Manager: Ms. Wang joined Voltronic at its
establishment in 2008. She previously worked as a manager at Centralion
Industrial.
nChih-Hsin Chin – Vice General Manager: Mr. Chin joined Voltronic in 2008.
Before joining the company, he worked as a manager at Phoenixtec Power.

Voltronic Power
Company description
Established in 2008 and listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 2014,
Voltronic is a Taiwan-based design and manufacturing service (DMS) provider
specializing in uninterrupted power supplies (UPS) and power-conversion
products. Voltronic adopts a pure-play ODM business model, which avoids
direct competition with customers and has helped the company establish

long-term relationships with most global Tier-1 UPS brands. The company is
headquartered in Taipei and operates manufacturing facilities in Taiwan,
China and Vietnam.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Voltronic, supported by an earnings recovery,
structural UPS outsourcing and emerging opportunities in AIDC power
infrastructure. Its customization capabilities and longstanding relationships
with western power vendors position it to capture additional outsourcing

orders and expand into higher power products. HVDC power racks offer a L-T
growth opportunity and potential re-rating catalyst. We view 2026E as
earnings trough, with a richer online UPS mix and operating leverage driving a
28% earnings rebound in 2027E.
Valuation
We value Voltronic using a forward target P/E of 25x our 2027E EPS to derive a
target price of NT$1050, broadly in line with its long term average forward P/E
since 2013. We believe forward P/E is appropriate given the company’s

established earnings base and the importance of product mix and operating
leverage to its profitability.
Risks
Key downside risks to our Buy rating and target price include: 1) Slower UPS
demand or outsourcing gain. 2) Less favorable product mix and margin
pressure. 3) Persistent PV inverter competition. 4) Weaker macro economy

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 126: 261014_daiwa_ ITEQ

Source PDF: all report pdf/261014_daiwa_ ITEQ.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We preview ITEQ’s 3Q26 results (due on 6 November). In our 
previous note, “Upgrading: strong beat in 2Q26 with improving outlook” (7 
August), we had underestimated the magnitude and durability of price hikes 
for E-glass and M4-and-below CCL. We raise our 2026-28E earnings by 
43-70% to reflect better-than-expected pricing and gross margin 
performance, which leads to our higher TP of TWD1,110 (from TWD600).

What’s the impact: Ongoing price hike of E-glass and M4-and-below 
CCL. This week, a major Japanese copper-clad laminate (CCL) vendor 
raised its entry-level CCL price by 200% to catch up with other global 
peers’ price hike level. One of the Chinese peers is also having a constant 
c.10% ASP hike on a monthly basis in 2026. We are increasingly confident 
in the management’s target for its blended price hike in 2H26. We now 
forecast c.30%/20% price hikes in 3Q26E and 4Q26E, respectively (vs. our 
previous estimates of 20%/10%). Looking ahead, we expect another 10% 
price hike per quarter in 1H27 (c.20% price hike in total), while pricing 
should gradually stabilise in 2H27 given the rising supply of E-glass, in our 
view. While the visibility towards 2H27 and 2028 remains relatively limited, 
we believe the key upside risks to our current forecast will be the stronger-
than-expected price hike magnitude and durability of the current up-cycle.

3Q26 results preview. After factoring in stronger-than-expected price 
hikes, ITEQ’s 3Q26 revenue grew c.35% QoQ, mainly driven by the 30% 
price hike despite utilisation rate staying at c.75%. We lift our gross margin 
forecast to c.30.3% (from 24.7% in our August note), in line with the August 
preliminary results’ pre-tax margin of 25.8%. With price hikes continuing, 
we expect the 4Q26E gross margin to expand to c.35%. Given disciplined 
capacity expansion through 2028, we expect the higher GM to be sustained 
through 2028. Beyond price hike, we believe the key longer-term growth 
drivers include: a) rising regular server demand and new platform adoption 
(using M7 grade CCL) in 2027; and b) M9 certification by more clients.

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 43-70% to factor in 
the price hikes. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating, and raise our 12M TP to 
TWD1,110 (from TWD600), based on an unchanged target PER of 22x 
(higher than the range during the past 12 months of 5-18x) applied to our 1-
year-forward EPS. While we expect the current upcycle will eventually ease 
starting from mid-2027, we remain positive on the near-term price outlook. 
Key downside risk: weaker-than-expected regular server demand.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 36-60% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, mainly due to our more aggressive view on ITEQ’s price hikes 
and margin profile.

14 October 2026

3Q26 results preview: better-than-expected price hikes

➢ Expecting better price hikes from 2H26 to 1H27 
➢ Spec upgrades driven by new CPU platform adoption in 2027 
➢ Reiterating our Buy (1) rating; raising our 12M TP to TWD1,110

Company profile 
ITEQ Corporation is a leading manufacturer of high performance copper clad laminate materials used 
for the fabrication of printed circuit boards and was founded in April of 1997. ITEQ’s products find use in 
many different application areas, such as computational and communications applications, Radio 
Frequency and Microwave devices, Automotive applications, and High Density Interconnect solutions.

Executive/board
quality 2
All board members implement the company’s operation faithfully and fulfil their obligations with
discipline and care. Independent directors obey related laws and company regulations to protect
the rights of the company and stockholders.
Capital management 2
ITEQ paid a cash DPS of TWD3 in 2025, representing a 72% payout ratio. We believe ITEQ will
sustain a similar payout level of c.75% over 2026-28E. We see its balance between investment
and dividend as appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 2 ITEQ’s sales to related parties were insignificant versus its total revenue.
S Supply chainmanagement 2 ITEQ requires all major raw material suppliers to comply with ESG, ethics, and regulatorystandards. Annual RBA audits were conducted for vendors, all rated A–C.
S Data security 1 ITEQ has established an ISO/IEC 27001-certified Information Security Management System,conducts quarterly training, and implements system recovery drills to ensure risk preparedness.

研 究  報 告                
貿聯-KY(3665.TT) 
產業：電子–其他電子 
正式合併 Interplex 財報，大幅挹注 HPC 產品營收 
 投資評價與建議 
正式併購Interplex，預估2026/2027年稅後EPS為70.58/128.86元，過往貿聯
本益比位於17~30倍區間，考量併購與光通訊專案逐步發酵，給予25倍PER。 
 重點評論分析 
正式併購Interplex集團Datacom業務，大幅挹注HPC營收：近期貿聯正式合
併Interplex集團資料通訊業務，並自2026年9月23日起納入合併報表。  
Interplex產品包含：伺服器機殼、機櫃、滑軌與CPU Socket等，併購有利提
升貿聯在單⼀機櫃之產值，另外有利切入過往較難打進供應鏈之CSP客戶。 
HPC和半導體設備仍為主要獲利引擎 ， 光通訊佈局逐步發酵 ：展望2026~2027
年 ， 營收動能主要來自 ： (1)Ethernet AEC規格逐漸從400G往800G迭代 ， 800G 
AEC主要採用客戶為xAI與AWS，1.6T AEC預計將於4Q26隨Nvidia Rubin新
平台開始正式出貨，ALC主動式光纜目前已有2個以上客戶表示願意採用，預
估2H27開始貢獻營收 ； (2)Vera Rubin機櫃功率突破300kW ， 帶動Power Whip
規格從60A提升至100A 、 機櫃內Busbar規格從1,400A提升至5,000Ａ ， 電源規
格提升帶動產品單價隨安培數增加而提升；(3)Rubin Ultra機櫃Power Rack設
計帶動Power Whip用量與ASP皆顯著增加 ， 預估產值成⻑2倍以上 ； (4)貿聯與
ams OSRAM合作開發microVCSEL互連方案，另外與SENKO和ficonTEC合作
全自動化光學互連與光纖製備流程，目前光通訊營收已佔整體營收約2~3%。 
 投資風險 
伺服器新平台出貨進度不如預期、原物料成本上漲。

### Page 2

2

| 營運概況  
2Q26毛利率重回成⻑軌道，HPC與半導體設備需求持續強勁，3Q26營收創高：2Q26營收季增11.6%至232.6億元，係
因HPC與半導體設備業務需求持續強勁，1Q26毛利率僅28.8%，主要受車用等非AI低毛利產品復甦導致產品組合改變
以及xAI因併購使其資料中心建置進度停滯 ， 導致延後拉貨AEC與power產品 ， 貿聯產能利用率下降亦影響毛利率 ， 2Q26
毛利率季增1.96ppts至30.7%，重回過往水準，係因客戶逐步恢復拉貨，稅後EPS為15.28元。貿聯8月營收月減7.7%至
88.9億元，係因歐洲地區歷經季節性暑期⻑假，9月營收月增5%至93.3億元，營收達成率101%，符合預期，係因xAI逐
步恢復拉貨以及AWS Trainium 3放量，3Q26營收季增19.8%至278.9億元，預估稅後EPS為19.12元。 
正式併購Interplex集團Datacom業務，大幅挹注HPC營收： 貿聯-KY於2026年6月宣布斥資8.5億美元收購新加坡
Interplex集團資料通訊業務，近期正式合併並自2026年9月23日起納入合併報表。Interplex營運總部位於新加坡，產能
遍及南亞與亞洲地區，包括：泰國、越南、⾺來⻄亞與中國等，其核心技術為精密金屬加工與機構件，主要產品包含：
伺服器機殼、機櫃、滑軌與CPU Socket等，另外僅Busbar產品線與貿聯重疊且同為Nvidia MGX合格供應商，併購除擴
張HPC營收規模外，亦提升貿聯在單⼀AI Server機櫃之產值，另外透過併購有利切入過往較難打進供應鏈之CSP客戶。
2025年Interplex營收約4億美元，佔貿聯2025年營收之17%，隨AI運算與推論需求增加，假設Interplex 2026和2027年
營收皆年增30%，預估可貢獻貿聯2026/2027年營收分別約39與200億元。 
HPC 和半導體設備仍為主要獲利引擎，光通訊佈局逐步發酵，成為「銅光並進」主要受惠者：展望 2026~2027 年，貿
聯營收動能主要來自：(1)隨 AI 頻寬需求增加，Ethernet AEC 規格逐漸從 400G 往 800G 迭代，預計 2026 年底前成為
主流規格，800G AEC 主要採用客戶為 xAI 與 AWS，其中 xAI 因 AEC 採用規格最高且⻑度最⻑，故 ASP 較高，1.6T 
AEC 預計將於 4Q26 隨 Nvidia Rubin 新平台開始正式出貨，由於 1.6T AEC 採用 8 條單通道 200G 之線材組裝，故組
裝難度提升且成本翻倍，因此 ASP 和毛利率皆優於過往規格，另外 PCIe AEC 則已開發完成，將隨 CSP 推出 PCIe 版本
Switch 放量，ALC 主動式光纜目前已有 2 個以上客戶表示願意採用，預估 2H27 開始貢獻營收；(2)隨 Vera Rubin 機櫃
功率突破 300kW，帶動 Power Whip 規格從 60A 提升至 100A、機櫃內 Busbar 規格從 1,400A 提升至 5,000Ａ，電源
規格提升帶動產品單價隨安培數增加而提升；(3)Rubin Ultra 機櫃可容納 576 顆 GPU 使單⼀機櫃功耗超過 500kW．由
於機櫃無法容納電源設備，故單獨拉出成為 Power Rack．帶動「Power Rack 至運算機櫃」電源連接需求大幅增加，預
估 Power Whip 之用量與 ASP 皆顯著增加 ， 預估產值成⻑超過 2 倍以上 ； (4)光通訊領域方面 ， 近期貿聯與 ams OSRAM
合作開發 AI scale-up microVCSEL 互連方案，另外持續與 SENKO 和 ficonTEC 合作全自動化的光學互連與光纖製備流
程，目前光通訊營收已佔公司整體營收約 2~3%，顯示光通訊產品成⻑⼒道強勁。綜上所述，預估 2026/2027 年營收
年增 49.5%/55.4%至 1,064.9/1,654.6 億元，稅後 EPS 為 70.58/128.86 元。

營收走勢圖

研究員/丁宥霖 yulin.ting@uni-psg.com 
       廖婉婷 TINA_LIAO@uni-psg.com

臻鼎 9 月營收 263.58 億元 ， 改寫單月新高，3Q26 營收 646.24 
億元，優於研究部原估的 607.09 億元，主因 AI 相關業務出貨
優於預期 ， 加上手機客戶進入新品旺季 ， 研究部據此上修 3Q26 稅
後 EPS 至 3.84 元（原估 3.47 元）。展望 4Q26，行動通訊旺
季延續， 加上 GPU 與 ASIC 新一代主板開始放量 ，營收與獲利將
續創新高 。2027 年伺服器與光模塊隨新平台出貨擴大及 1.6T 成
為主流， 營收可望各再增逾百億元；IC 載板亦受惠供給持續吃緊
及新產能陸續開出 ， 帶動 AI 相關營收占比突破三成 。 綜上所述 ，
研究部上修臻鼎 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 至 12.11 元（+99.6% 
YoY，原估 11.81 元）及 26.14 元（+118.6% YoY，原估 22.91 
元）。基於臻鼎產品橫跨軟板、類載板、HDI、HLC 與 IC 載板，
可為客戶提供一站式解決方案 ， 並提前掌握關鍵材料與設備產能 ，
在高階 PCB 供給吃緊下擴產得以大量開出搶奪市占，研究部維
持以 2027 年獲利為評價基礎及 28 倍本益比，目標價上調至 
730 元，維持買進投資評等。

重點摘要： 
1. 9 月營收改寫新高，上修 2H26 獲利預估 
臻鼎 9 月營收 263.58 億元（+27.5% MoM、+36.1% YoY），改寫
單月新高 ， 其中伺服器 、 光模塊與 IC 載板合計營收年增逾三倍 ，
行動通訊亦呈雙位數月增 與年增 。3Q26 營收 646.24 億元
（+33.4% QoQ、+36.4% YoY），達研究部原估的 106.4%，明顯優
於預期 ， 主因 GPU 新一代主板進入量產 、1.6T 光模塊出貨放大 ，
加上手機客戶進入新品旺季。考量 AI 相關產品占比提升，研究
部預估 3Q26 毛利率為 25.5%（+2.4ppt QoQ、+3.5ppt YoY，原
估 24.7%），稅後 EPS 上修至 3.84 元（+81.9% QoQ 、+81.9% 
YoY，原估 3.47 元） 。展望 4Q26，行動通訊旺季延續，加上 GPU 
與 ASIC 新一代主板放量，研究部預估 4Q26 營收 736.13 億元
（+13.9% QoQ、+29.4% YoY），稅後 EPS 4.75 元（+23.8% QoQ、
+70.4% YoY，原估 4.86 元），營收與獲利續創新高。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. AI 伺服器主板成為最主要成長動能 
AI 伺服器主板隨 GPU 與 ASIC 平台升級，晶片封裝尺寸、I/O 
數與傳輸速率同步提升，主板須在有限面積內完成更多高速訊號
的扇出與繞線。 傳統 HLC 以一次壓合搭配機械鑽孔與背鑽 ，層間
連接密度已近極限；高階 HDI 則以多次壓合與雷射盲孔逐層堆
疊，線路更細、訊號路徑更短，可降低高速傳輸損耗，帶動 GPU 
平台 OAM 等主板設計由 HLC 轉向 HDI。HDI 須投入大量雷射鑽
孔機與多次壓合產能，在設備與材料交期拉長下，全球高階  AI 
PCB 產能缺口約 15–20%， 訂單已排至 2028 年，2027–2028 年
產品報價亦將持續上調。臻鼎提早取得全球逾五成高階雷射鑽孔
機產能 ， 在建 22 座新廠中半數投入 HDI、 伺服器與光模塊產品 ，
在同業擴產受限下得以持續承接訂單，帶動伺服器 HDI 比重由 
1H26 的約五成於 2H26 明顯提升；繼 ASIC 專案今年大量出貨
後，GPU 平台新一代主板亦自 4Q26 起放量。研究部預估臻鼎伺
服器營收 2026、2027 年各增加逾 100 億元 ， 為未來兩年最主要
成長動能。

3. 臻鼎可望躍居 1.6T 光模塊 PCB 龍頭 
AI 伺服器傳輸速率由 800G 升級至 1.6T，單通道速率倍增使線
路須更細 、 阻抗控制更精準 ， 傳統減成法已難以達到 ， 光模塊 PCB 
因此改採 mSAP 製程。臻鼎過去為手機類載板主要供應商，具備 
mSAP 大量生產經驗 ； 在高階設備交期拉長 、 關鍵材料供給吃緊下 ，
臻鼎已掌握市場上多數高階設備產能並持續擴充，同時提前鎖定
上游材料，得以在淮安與泰國同步擴充光模塊產能，研究部推估
臻鼎可望在 2027 年 1.6T 世代成為全球光模塊 PCB 龍頭。隨 
1.6T 出貨占比由 2026 年的約五成提升至 2027 年逾七成，研
究部預估臻鼎光模塊營收由 2025 年約 9 億元躍升至 2026 年
逾 100 億元，2027 年再增至逾 250 億元。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

4. ABF 與 BT 同步吃緊，新產能與轉單推升載板營收 
AI 晶片封裝面積與層數持續放大，ABF 載板用量與製程難度同
步提升，惟新廠建置期長、上游材料吃緊，供需缺口持續擴大；
同業亦將部分 BT 產能轉往 ABF，使 BT 載板同步出現缺貨，兩
者報價自下半年起持續上漲。臻鼎 ABF 載板技術規格已達最大 
158×158mm、 最高 28 層 ， 深圳一廠 1Q26 轉盈並將於年底滿載 ，
二廠 1Q27 開始貢獻，第二園區 2028 年起約每年開出一座新
廠，且新產能幾乎全由國際客戶預先包下，高雄廠亦已量產先進
封裝相關載板。BT 載板雖無新建廠計畫， 但近期承接同業轉單並
調漲報價 ， 加上產品與客戶組合持續優化 ， 可望維持雙位數成長 。
隨 ABF 產能接續開出，研究部預估 ABF 占載板營收比重將由 
2025 年的 35% 提升至 2027 年約七成，IC 載板營收 2026、
2027 年分別成長 99.2% 及 89.0%。

5. 高階新產能陸續開出，產品組合優化推升毛利率 
臻鼎 2026 年資本支出逾 800 億元，2027 年維持相當水準，廠
房數將由目前的 28 座擴增至 2030 年底的 59 座。新產能自 
2027 年起陸續開出：淮安 6 座新廠於 2026 年底完工後進入裝
機，HD 園區預計 3M27 投產、2Q27 大批量產；泰國新廠於 4Q26 
起試產，1H27 進入小量生產 ； 深圳第三園區與載板第二園區則預
計自 2028 年起接棒 （表二） 。新廠初期稼動率與良率仍在爬坡，
加上 2027 年折舊費用預計較 2026 年再增加 20% 至 30%，短
期將稀釋毛利率；惟新增產能以 AI 伺服器、光模塊與 IC 載板
為主，隨 AI 相關營收占比由 2025 年的 11.7% 提升至 2027 
年突破三成，研究部預估臻鼎毛利率將由 2026 年的 24.5% 提
升至 2027 年的 29.3%。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  5

6. 上修 2026、2027 年獲利預估 
2026 年 AI 相關業務為主要成長動能 ，研究部預估伺服器 、光模
塊與其他營收成長 269.0%，IC 載板成長 99.2%，行動通訊受惠
摺疊機新品成長 6.9%，電腦消費則與 2025 年持平，AI 相關營
收占比將由 2025 年的 11.7% 提升至 25.9%。研究部預估 2026 
年營收 2,273.96 億元（+24.6% YoY，原估 2,267.70 億元），
毛利率由 24.1% 上修至 24.5%（+4.7ppt YoY ），稅後 EPS 由 
11.81 元上修至 12.11 元（+99.6% YoY）。 
展望 2027 年，伺服器與光模塊隨新平台出貨擴大及 1.6T 成為
主流，營收合計成長逾一倍，IC 載板受惠 AI 客戶訂單放量、
ABF 比重提升及報價調漲，成長 89.0%，行動通訊與電腦消費則
分別維持中、低個位數成長。在高毛利產品占比提升、費用率隨
營收規模擴大回落，抵銷新廠折舊增加下，研究部預估 2027 年
營收 3,158.99 億元（+38.9% YoY，原估 3,083.20 億元），毛
利率提升至 29.3%（+4.8ppt YoY，原估 28.1%） ，營益率提升至 
17.9%（原估 14.8%） ， 稅後 EPS 上修至 26.14 元（+118.6% YoY，
原估 22.91 元）。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投

陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非

## Document 215: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기 환율 부진에도 영업이익 100조는 넘긴다 
삼성전자 26년 3분기 실적은 이전 전망치를 하회할 전망이다. 매출액은 195.2조원으
로 이전 분기 대비 13.8% 증가하고, 영업이익은 101.7조원으로 13.6% 증가할 것으
로 예상한다. 영업이익률은 52.1%로 전 분기 대비 0.1%p 하락할 전망이다. 원/달러 
환율 영향이 주요 변수로 작용한 것으로 추정한다. 2분기에 집행되지 않았던 성과급 
일부가 3분기에 반영된 영향도 있을 것으로 예상한다. DS 부문은 매출액 149.9조원, 
영업이익은 101.8조원으로 추정하고, 디스플레이 매출액 8.8조원, 영업이익 0.9조
원, MX/네트워크는 매출액 34.3조원, 영업적자 1.1조원으로 예상한다. VD/가전도 
영업적자 규모가 커질 것으로 예상한다.  
주주환원은 주가 상승을 견인할 것 
8월 21일 이후 주주환원에 대한 관심이 커졌다. 자사주 규모는 크지 않겠지만 배당
을 통한 주주환원 규모는 사상 최대 규모일 것으로 예상한다. 글로벌 대표 IT업체와 
비교해도 손색없는 수준이다. 메모리 업체의 주주환원 규모가 100조원 넘게 집행된 
적이 없어서 주가에 어떠한 영향을 미칠 지가 관전 포인트인데 긍정적 역할을 할 것
으로 기대한다. 자사주를 통한 EPS 개선은 아니지만, 안정적이고 지속적인 규모의 
주주환원은 주가를 한층 더 높은 수준에서 평가 받는 계기가 될 것으로 판단한다.  
현저한 저평가 구간, 이전 전망 유지 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 메모리 공급 부족 상황이 장기화될 
것으로 전망하고, 이로 인해 수익성은 현재 수준에서 유지되고, 이익 규모는 안정적
으로 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 현저히 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 목
표주가는 460,000원을 유지한다.

### Page 4

삼성전자 (005930)

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
반도체 Check Point 
1. 차별화된 주주환원 전략. 배경은 안정적 이익 규모 
2026년에는 대대적인 규모의 주주환원이 예상된다. 100 조원 내외 수준으로 
예상하는데 상당 부분은 배당으로 집행될 것으로 추정한다. 배당성향이 25%가 유지될 
것으로 예상하기 때문이다. 자사주 매입 여력은 20조원 내외 수준일 것으로 예상한다. 
주주환원은 주가 밸류 상승에 기여할 것으로 기대한다. 일회성이 아니고 장기적으로 
높은 수준의 주주환원이 가능하다는 것은 싸이클 변동에 따른 이익의 변동성이 
낮다는 의미이기도 하다. 
2. AI 모델 개발 속도 조절. 그래도 메모리 수요를 줄일 수는 없다 
Anthropic CEO는 현재 AI 개발 속도가 지나치게 빨라서 인간의 능력을 뛰어 넘는 
임계점에 이를 가능성이 높아져서 개발 속도를 늦출 필요가 있다고 언급했다. 이를 
Meta, Google, SpaceX 수장들이 공감했다. 우선 이들의 공감으로 AI 모델의 개발 
속도가 둔화될 것으로 보고 있지 않다. 또한, 이를 가정하더라도 향후 AI 모델은 
Agent AI로 진화할 것으로 보고 있다. 이에 따른 메모리에 대한 필요성은 급격히 
증가할 것으로 기대한다. 
3. 자신감 넘치는 HBM  
HBM4가 2026년 하반기부터 주력 제품이 될 것으로 전망한다. 26 년 3분기에는 
비중이 40% 내외, 4 분기에는 60%를 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 연초 기대 대비로는 
낮은 수준이다. 삼성전자의 점유율만 보면 연초 대비 높은 수준의 점유율이 예상된다.  
HBM4 물량이 예상보다 늦게 본격화되면서 2026년 HBM 시장 규모는 이전 전망 
대비 축소될 전망이다. HBM4도 늦은 출시로 연내 생산 물량은 이전 전망치인 130억 
Gb을 하회할 것으로 예상한다.  
2027년 HBM4 가격이 최근에 확정이 되었고, 이를 통해서 DRAM 수준의 수익성 
확보가 가능할 것으로 기대한다. 수익성 발목을 잡았던 HBM은 2027년 물량부터는 
영업이익 개선의 변수가 될 것으로 전망한다.

장기적 주주환원은 
안정적 이익이 뒷받침 
Agent AI는 메모리 
수요를 증가시킨다 
HBM4 안정적 진입한 
것으로 판단

동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

## Document 227: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) Long Trading 구간LG에너지솔루션(373220) Long Trading 구간Long Trading 구간 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) Long Trading 구간LG에너지솔루션(373220) Long Trading 구간Long Trading 구간 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

LG에너지솔루션 (373220)

Company Brief 
이차전지/석유화학 
Analyst 노우호 
wooho.rho@meritz.co.kr 
RA 황현식 
hyeonsig.hwang@meritz.co.kr Long Trading 구간  
3Q26P 잠정실적 발표 이후: ESS ↑, BEV ↓  
LG에너지솔루션의 3Q26P 잠정실적 발표 이후 배터리 섹터는 11/3 미국 중간선거
를 전후로 Trading Long 투자의견이 유효하다. 미국 중간선거 이후 특정정당 우세와 
별개로, 미국 Ex-China ESS 공급망 프리미엄은 유효하다. 동사 역시 그간의 ESS 
사업 난제(단위당 수율, 팩 병목)는 점진적 해소될 점이다. 반면 시장의 BEV 반등 기
대감에 동의하지 않는다. EU 프랑스와 독일의 내연기관 퇴출시점 지연 움직임 및 미
국 연비규제 정책 폐지 결정으로 전통 완성차 고객사들향 BEV 배터리 출하 부진은 
여전하겠다. 미국의 BEV 정책 변화시점은 28.11월 대선으로 지연될 전망이다.

ESS 모멘텀: 한국 제3차 ESS 중앙계약시장 사업자 선정 모멘텀 
대다수 시장 참여자들은 2026년 연말까지 LG에너지솔루션의 미국 ESS 60Gwh  규
모의 잔여 수주 달성 가시성에 집중한다. 해당 ESS 사업 수주 가이던스 관건은 시기
보다 방향성이다. 단, 당사가 주목하는 연말 모멘텀은 한국 정부향 제3차 ESS 중앙
계약시장 사업자 선정으로 판단한다. 3차 규모는 1.13Gw로 1&2차 계약 규모(합산 
1.13Gw) 대비 확대되었다. 또한 언론보도상 LG에너지솔루션은 해당 수주를 위한 핵
심 4대 소재 등 전 Value-chain 국산화를 선언한 바 있다. 해당 수주 확정시, 동사
를 비롯한 해당 Value-chain에 긍정적 모멘텀이 예상된다.

투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 51만원 제시   
26년 연중 지정학 Risk, 에너지 대전환 영향 등에 전력 인프라 수요는 꾸준할 전망
이다. 향후 국내 이차전지 산업은 ‘L’ Shaped Cycle 전망, 반면 동사는 스마트카 T사
향 원통형 전지 출하량 증가, ESS 제조업 이상으로 변모하는 과정으로 산업 평균 성
장률 Outperform 가능하다. 투자의견 Buy 유지, 적정주가는 추정치 상향에 51만원
을 제시한다.

Meritz Research  2026. 10. 15

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 510,000원

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속LG에너지솔루션(373220) 원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속원-_fba73583.pdf

LG에너지솔루션 
(373220)

원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속 Company Brief

3Q26 원/달러 환율 하락에도 불구하고 영업이익 시장 기대치 상회 전망 
동사의 3Q26 실적은 매출 8.8조원(+45% YoY, + 16% QoQ), 영업이익 3,690억원(-
39% YoY, +226% QoQ)을 기록하며 원/달러 환율 하락으로 매출은 시장 기대치를 
소폭 하회하겠으나 영업이익은 상회할 것으로 전망된다. AMPC 는 미국 ESS 생산량 
증가와 GM 얼티엄셀즈 재가동에 힘입어 전분기 대비 약 57% 증가한 3,790억원으로 
추정된다. 미국 상호관세 위법 판결에 따른 관세 환급금이나 완성차 업체들의 최소 
구매 물량 미달 보상금 반영 여부에 따라 추가적인 이익 개선 가능성도 존재한다. ESS 
부문은 전분기에서 이연된 물량의 출하가 본격화되면서 3Q26 매출이 전분기 대비 약 
38% 증가할 것으로 예상된다. 특히 미국 ESS 생산라인의 가동 안정화와 AMPC 수혜 
확대에 힘입어 수익성도 점진적으로 개선될 것으로 보인다. 자동차전지 부문도 
3Q26부터 회복세가 강화될 것으로 예상된다. 유럽향 물량 회복과 GM 얼티엄셀즈 
재가동으로 매출이 전분기 대비 큰 폭으로 증가하고, 고정비 부담 완화로 적자폭도 
뚜렷하게 축소될 전망이다. 소형전지 부문은 주요 고객사인 Tesla의 판매 호조에 따른 
안정적인 출하 증가로 매출 회복과 수익성 방어가 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 
4Q26 대규모 ESS 신규 수주 기대 
전세계 ESS 시장은 신재생 에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 힘입어 빠르게 
성장하고 있다. 시장조사기관 SNE리서치에 따르면 1H26 전세계 ESS 배터리 
출하량은 461GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 71% 증가했다. 이 중 북미 출하량은 
76GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 83% 증가했으며, 분기별로는 1Q26 35GWh(+91% 
YoY), 2Q26 41GWh(+76% YoY) 를 기록했다. 통상 하반기에 출하가 집중되는 
계절성을 감안하면 올해 북미 ESS 배터리 출하량은 전년 대비 약 70% 증가한 
170~180GWh에 이를 것으로 전망된다. 연초 동사가 예상한 올해 북미 ESS 수요 
성장률 10% 초반 수준을 크게 상회할 가능성이 높은 상황이다. 올해 동사의 ESS 
신규 수주 목표는 약 90GWh이며, 현재까지 약 30GWh의 수주를 확정한 것으로 
파악된다. 이에 시장 일각에서는 연간 수주 목표 달성 여부에 대한 우려가 제기되고 
있다. 그러나 당사가 파악한 바에 따르면 대규모 ESS 신규 수주가 4Q26에 집중될 
것으로 예상된다. 특히 북미 ESS 수요의 가파른 증가세와 투자세액공제(48E)에 
연계된 PFE 공급망 규제 시행은 동사의 수주 확보에 우호적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
매수 투자의견과 목표주가 유지 
동사에 대한 매수 투자의견과 목표주가를 유지한다. 향후 국내 배터리 셀 업체들의 
북미 ESS 신규 수주가 지속적으로 확대될 전망이다. 미국 ESS 시장에서는 신재생 
에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 따른 수요 증가, 중국 공급망에 대한 규제 
강화가 맞물리면서 국내 업체들에 우호적인 사업 환경이 조성되고 있다. 동사 역시 
ESS 중심의 생산라인 전환과 가동률 상승이 본격화될 경우 매출 성장과 함께 고정비 
부담 완화, AMPC 수혜 확대에 따른 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 이를 바탕으로 동사의 
영업이익은 2026년 약 0.8조원에서 2028년 약 5.1조원으로 가파르게 증가할 것으로 
전망된다. ESS의 역할이 신재생 에너지 연계용 저장장치를 넘어 AIDC의 안정적인 
전력 공급을 지원하는 핵심 인프라로 확대되고 있다는 점에도 주목할 필요가 있다. 
이에 따라 동사의 밸류에이션 역시 기존 EV 중심의 평가에서 벗어나 전력 인프라 
산업의 성장성을 반영하는 방향으로 재평가되어야 한다고 판단된다. 업종 내에서 실적 
개선 가시성과 정책 수혜가 뚜렷한 동사에 대한 비중 확대 전략이 유효하다.

당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상 
산업추천 투자등급  
시가총액기준 산업별 시장비중대비 보유비중의 변화를 추천하는 것임 
 Overweight(비중확대) 
 Neutral(중립) 
 Underweight(비중축소)

하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대 
예정보다 빠른 SK 하이닉스 자사주 매입 
SK스퀘어의 자회사인 SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 20일부터 11월 19일까지 자사주 40
조원(2,407만주, 3.3%)을 매입 예정이다. 9월 29일까지 1,715만주, 예정 매입수량 
대비 71.3%를 매입 완료하였다. 매입 초기 매일 65만주 매입하다가 9월 10일부터는 
60만주를 매입하고 있는데, 이러한 추세가 유지된다면 10월 중순경 자사주 매입이 마
무리될 예정이다. 자사주 매입 후 소각 예정으로 소각이 완료되면 SK스퀘어의 SK하아
닉스에 대한 지분율은 20.0%에서 20.7%로 상승하게 된다.

자사주 매입 이후 추가적인 주주환원에 대한 기대 
SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 19일 정책기간(‘25년~’27년) 동안 발생하는 누적 FCF의 
50% 이상을 주주환원 하는 주주환원 정책을 발표한 바 있다. 26년 SK하이닉스의 예
상 FCF는 164조원(컨센서스 기준)으로 50%를 주주환원 할 경우 자사주 매입 금액
(40 조원)을 제외한 주주환원 재원은 42 조원이다. 잔여 주주환원 재원이 모두 배당으
로 지급된다면 SK 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후 SK 스퀘어의 지분율은 20.7%이므로 
SK스퀘어의 배당수입도 8.7조원에 달한다. 지난 3월 SK스퀘어는 ‘26년~’28년 경
상배당수입의 30% 이상을 주주에게 환원하는 정책을 발표했다. 보수적으로 30%만 
가정하더라도 SK스퀘어의 배당재원은 2.6조원(현 시가총액 대비 1.8%)이다. SK스퀘
어는 연초 주주총회에서 배당가능이익 확보 차원에서 자본준비금 5 조 8,900억원을 
이익잉여금으로 전입한 바 있다. 이는 26년 재무제표가 확정되는 27년 3월 이후 배당
가능이익으로 편입되므로 SK스퀘어의 주주환원 확대가 기대된다.

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 185 만원(유지) 
SK스퀘어의 순자산 가치 중 98% 이상이 SK하이닉스 지분가치이다. SK하이닉스는 
자사주 매입 이후 17.7% 주가가 상승했으나, SK 스퀘어 주가는 11.4% 상승했다. SK
하이닉스 자사주 매입·소각은 SK 스퀘어의 보유 지분가치 상승뿐 아니라 지분율도 상
승하기 때문에 자사주가 소각될 경우 SK스퀘어의 주가가 탄력적으로 움직일 가능성이 
높다. SK스퀘어 현재 NAV 대비 할인율은 43.7%, 12MF PBR 1.4배로 7월 이후 주
가 조정으로 밸류에이션 매력도 높아졌다. 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 185만원을 유지
한다.

## Document 235: AIがサイバー攻撃を自動化する時代、サイバー株は上がるのか ～攻撃能力の急伸と企業業績・株価から検証する～

Source PDF: all report pdf/AIがサイバー攻撃を自動化する時代、サイバー株は上がるのか ～攻撃能力の急伸と企業業績・株価から検証する～.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Research Report

2026 年 10 月 6 日

1/6 
AI がサイバー攻撃を自動化する時代、サイバー株
は上がるのか 
～攻撃能力の急伸と企業業績・株価から検証する～ 
 資産運用調査部 主席研究員  柏村 祐

（要旨） 
○ AI によるサイバー攻撃の自動化・高度化が現実のものとなっている。豪政府システムへのAI
侵入事件などが示すように、AI は与えられた目的を達成する過程で、許可された範囲を超え
てシステムにアクセスしうることが明らかになった。国内でも 9 月下旬以降、幅広い業種の
企業で不正アクセスの公表が相次いでいる。 
○ AI エージェントのサイバー課題解決能力はここ数年で急速に向上しており、サイバーセキュ
リティ企業の売上高・利益も足元で好調に推移している。 
○ 一方で、高度な AI モデルの登場は既存のサイバーセキュリティ製品の陳腐化リスクへの懸
念を生み、関連株が下落するというパラドックスも起きている。「サイバー株なら何でも上
がる」という安易な連想買いには注意が必要である。 
○ 個人投資家は、サイバーセキュリティセクター全体を丸ごと買うのではなく、「AIネイティ
ブ」なサイバーセキュリティ企業や、AIを活用した防衛技術を持つ巨大テック企業（メガテ
ック）への選別投資が求められる。

1. 豪政府システムへの「AI侵入事件」が鳴らす警鐘 
2026 年9月、オーストラリアのアルバニージー首相は、同国の公的医療保険制度（Medicare）
の統計ポータルサイトに、OpenAIのAIエージェントが無断で侵入していたと発表した。注目すべ
きは、これが悪意あるハッカーによるサイバー攻撃ではなく、OpenAIの研究チームがAIに調査を
実行させる過程で起きた点だ。AIは調査中にアクセス制限に直面すると、自ら制限を回避する方
法を見つけ出し、非公開のファイルにまでアクセスしたという。この事例は、AIが与えられた目
的を達成する過程で、許可された範囲を超えてシステムにアクセスしうることを示した。 
サイバー攻撃の脅威は、海の向こうだけの話ではない。日本でも9月下旬以降、物流、交通、
小売、カーシェアリングなど幅広い業種の企業が、不正アクセスによる個人情報流出の可能性を
相次いで公表している。これらの攻撃にAIが用いられたかどうかは明らかになっていないが、サ
イバー攻撃の影響が業種を問わず及んでいることは確かだ。政府も10月1 日から、能動的サイバ

### Page 2

2/6 
ー防御の一環として、重大なサイバー攻撃を未然に防ぐため、警察や自衛隊が攻撃元のサーバー
等にアクセスし、無害化措置を講じることを可能にした。 
AIがサイバー攻撃を自動化・高度化させる時代が到来しつつある。企業や政府の防御需要は急
増し、サイバーセキュリティ企業には特需がもたらされると期待される。個人投資家の間の「AI
の次なる恩恵銘柄はサイバー株」という見方にも、一定の説得力がある。しかし、 「サイバー株な
ら何でも上がる」と考えるのは早計である。AIによる攻撃の自動化・高度化は、攻撃件数を増や
すだけでなく、従来のセキュリティ製品や防御手法の有効性そのものを揺るがしかねないから
だ。本稿では、AIの攻撃能力の現在地と、それがサイバーセキュリティ企業の業績および株価に
与える影響を、データに基づいて検証する。そのうえで、 「サイバー株＝安全確実な成長株」とい
う見方をどう見直すべきか、個人投資家に向けて投資戦略を考えるための新たな視点を提示した
い。

2. AIの攻撃能力急伸とサイバーセキュリティ企業の業績・株価への影響 
(1) AIは人間なしでどこまで攻撃できるのか 
AIエージェントのサイバー攻撃関連能力は急速に高まっている。スタンフォード大学人間中心
AI研究所（Stanford HAI）が公表した「AI Index Report 2026」によれば、AIは人間が逐次指示
を与えなくても、専門的なサイバー課題を自律的に解ける水準に達しつつある。 
同レポートで紹介されている「Cybench」は、暗号、Webセキュリティ、リバースエンジニアリ
ング、フォレンジック、エクスプロイトなど、40の専門レベルのサイバーセキュリティ課題を評
価するベンチマークである。資料1 は、人間のガイドなし（unguided）でAIが課題を解決できた
割合の推移を示している。これを見ると、2024年時点ではわずか15％であった解決率が、2025年
には 82％へと急伸し、2026年には93％に達している。2年間で 78ポイントの上昇であり、AIが
自律的にサイバー課題を解く能力が大きく向上していることがわかる。

### Page 3

3/6 
資料 1 AI エージェントの自律的なサイバー課題解決能力は急伸している 
（注）サイバーセキュリティのベンチマーク「Cybench」における、人間のガイドなし（unguided）での課題解決率（％）の推移。
Cybench は暗号、Web セキュリティ、リバースエンジニアリング、フォレンジック、エクスプロイトなど 40 の専門レベル課題を評価
するもの。 
（出所）Stanford HAI, “AI Index Report 2026”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

(2) サイバー需要は企業の売上・利益に届いているのか 
こうしたAIによるサイバー攻撃の脅威が高まるなか、防御側であるサイバーセキュリティ企業
への需要拡大が期待される。実際、その需要はすでに企業業績に表れている。 
Nasdaqが公表した「Q1 2026 HXRXL Cybersecurity Commentary」によれば、サイバーセキュリ
ティ関連銘柄で構成される「Nasdaq ISE Cyber Security Select Index（HXRXL） 」構成企業の
2026 年第1 四半期決算は極めて好調であった。同レポートによると、構成企業の売上高は前年同
期比で 11％増加し、純利益は同29.3％の増加を記録しており、売上高以上に純利益が大きく伸び
ていることが確認できる。さらに、市場予想に対する結果をまとめた資料2を見ても、売上高で
予想を上回った企業は指数ウェイトの92.2％を占め、純利益に至っては100％の企業が予想を上
回った。サイバーセキュリティ需要の強さは、売上高と利益の双方に表れている。

### Page 4

4/6 
資料 2 サイバーセキュリティ企業の業績は市場予想を大きく上回る 
（注）Nasdaq ISE Cyber Security Select Index（HXRXL）構成企業の 2026 年第 1 四半期決算の集計結果。市場予想に対する売上
高および純利益の予想超え・予想未達の企業数（指数ウェイトベース）、ならびに平均上振れ・下振れ率（％）を示す。 
（出所）Nasdaq, “Q1 2026 HXRXL Cybersecurity Commentary”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

(3) それでもサイバー株は上がるのか 
業績が好調であれば株価も上昇すると考えるのが自然だが、 「AIによる攻撃高度化＝サイバー株
上昇」とは必ずしも言い切れない。AIは防御需要を増やす一方で、既存のサイバーセキュリティ
製品を陳腐化させるとの懸念も生んでいるからだ。 
国際決済銀行（BIS）が2026 年7月に公表したレポート「A Mythos moment? Frontier AI and 
cyber risk」は、このパラドックスを指摘している。2026年 4月にAnthropicが高度なサイバー攻
撃能力を持つAIモデル「Mythos」を発表した際、サイバーセキュリティ関連株は大きく下落し
た。資料3 は、Mythos発表前を0とした場合のサイバーセキュリティ関連株の累積異常リターン
（CAR）を示している。発表後、サイバーセキュリティETF（IHAK）や主要企業（CRWD、PANWな
ど）のCARは軒並みマイナスに転じ、一部の系列ではグラフ上で約−30％の大幅なマイナスを記録
した。BISは、この市場反応について「新しいAIモデルの登場により、既存のサイバーセキュリテ
ィ製品やサービスが陳腐化する（obsolescence）という投資家の懸念を反映している」との解釈
を紹介している。つまり、AIの進化はサイバーセキュリティ市場全体を拡大させる可能性が高い
ものの、同時にゲームチェンジを引き起こし、旧来の技術やサービスに依存する企業の競争力を
急速に奪うリスクを孕んでいる。

### Page 5

5/6 
資料 3 高度な AI モデルの発表後、サイバーセキュリティ関連株は下落した 
（注）2026 年 4 月の高度な AI モデル「Mythos」発表前後の、サイバーセキュリティ関連株の累積異常リターン（CAR、％）。発表前
を 0 として推移を示している。「Glasswing partners」は CrowdStrike（CRWD）および Palo Alto Networks（PANW）を指す。 
（出所）Bank for International Settlements, “BIS Bulletin No. 129: A Mythos moment? Frontier AI and cyber risk”をもとに第一ラ
イフ資産運用経済研究所作成

3. AI時代のサイバー株投資～「陳腐化リスク」を回避する新戦略～ 
第 2 章で見てきたように、AI はサイバー攻撃能力を飛躍的に高めている。また、足元のサイバ
ーセキュリティ企業の業績も好調である。しかし、最先端のAIモデルが登場した際には、市場は
既存のサイバーセキュリティ製品の「陳腐化リスク」に敏感に反応し、関連株は大きく売り込ま
れた。ここからいえるのは、サイバーセキュリティ市場全体は今後も拡大が見込まれるものの、
企業間の「勝ち負け」はこれまで以上に鮮明になるということだ。こうした環境変化のなか、個
人投資家には従来の投資アプローチを根本から見直すことが求められる。 
第一に、サイバーセキュリティセクター全体を丸ごと買うアプローチ（テーマ型ETFやインデ
ックスファンドなど）には注意が必要である。市場全体が成長しても、AIの進化に取り残され、
陳腐化して淘汰される旧世代の企業をポートフォリオに抱え込むリスクが高まるからだ。足元の
業績や過去の市場シェアだけで安心することはできない。第二に、個別銘柄を選別する際は、 「AI
ネイティブ」なサイバーセキュリティ企業であるかどうかが最大の焦点となる。攻撃側がAI を駆
使する以上、防御側もAIをシステムの中心に据え、未知の脅威を自律的に検知・遮断できるプラ
ットフォームを持っていなければ対抗できない。レガシーな製品を販売している企業ではなく、
クラウドベースで膨大なデータをAI で解析し、防御機能を継続的に高度化できる企業を見極める
必要がある。第三に、サイバー防衛の投資対象を「セキュリティ専業企業」以外にも広げること
である。 
最先端のAI モデルを開発し、強固なクラウドインフラを持つ巨大テック企業（メガテック）
は、自社のAI を活用した防衛技術を、顧客向けの強力なセキュリティサービスとして提供し始め

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
ている。豊富な資金力とAI 開発力を持つメガテックを、サイバー防衛の担い手として評価し、ポ
ートフォリオの中核に据えることも、AI時代の投資戦略として有効な選択肢となるだろう。

## Document 252: Allianz_2026-10-09_Toasted, not burnt France s bond sell-off and the road to the 2027 election_99a3202d

Source PDF: all report pdf/Allianz_2026-10-09_Toasted, not burnt France s bond sell-off and the road to the 2027 election_99a3202d.pdf
Report source: Allianz Trade
Date: 2026-10-09
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

In Summary 
• France is the hardest hit in the global bond sell-off, mostly due to its own fiscal and political 
weakness. Since the Hormuz crisis began, 10-year OAT yields have risen 150bps (US +115bps, 
Italy +132bps, Germany +83bps) as the prolonged energy crisis exacerbates fiscal concerns in 
a politically fragile environment. Franc e’ fiscal deficits have remained above 5% of GDP for 
four consecutive years. Debt has risen from 98% of GDP in 2019 to over 119% in Q2 2026, and 
the 2026 deficit is now expected at -5.4% against a -5% initial target. 
• A "short Europe" trade amplifies the move and spreads it to the rest of the Eurozone while 
the ECB looks the other way. Foreign banks and non-banks hold 36% of OATs and Japanese 
investors about 5% (EUR137bn), exposing France to the repatriation trade and price-sensitive 
investors. Speculative shorts, hedge -fund asset -swap unwinds and thin liquidity feed a 
negative loop, w ith market depth more than 50% below normal and the OAT spread over 
Bunds peaking at 150bp s. The speed reflects a positioning shock, not a pure repricing of 
fundamentals. Long -term investors are sitting out ahead of the elections, given budget 
uncertainty and the sensitivity of OATs to political fragility. Spanish and Italian spreads are 
about 20bps wider, bank CDS are widening and French and Italian three -month bills are 
converging toward the ECB's marginal lending rate. Fragmentation is far from 2012 or 2020 
levels, but the ingredients are in place. The ECB has not yet intervened and may limit  itself to 
verbal intervention, while continuing QT and pushing through two more hikes (+50bps).  
• Baseline: A watered -down budget, but the fiscal arithmetic stays heavy for France .  
We expect GDP growth of +0.5% in 2026 and +0.9% in 2027. The Assembly votes on the budget 
on 20 and 27 October, and we expect a budget by year -end through ordinances or Article 
49.3, keeping the 2027 deficit at -5.3% of GDP. This should contain fiscal slippage and stabilize 
financial conditions, with OAT spreads in the 120 -150bps range. Higher rates  (+100bps) will 
feed through over the next three to six months, adding EUR25bn (0.8% of GDP) in gross 
interest expenses for households, non -financial firms and the government combined. The 
debt-stabilizing deficit is -2.7% of GDP, which requires savings of more than  EUR80bn, plus 
EUR15-20bn a year to stop social and interest spending from widening the deficit. This takes 
credible fiscal measures (better tax mix, higher retirement age) and growth -enhancing 
reforms. 
• Adverse scenarios: France-centered crisis or Eurozone crisis? The Middle East situation is far 
from contained; oil prices could stay above USD100/bbl for another six months , leading to a 
further global bond sell -off hitting France the hardest, tightening financial conditions and 
jeopardizing deficit reduction. Domestically, opposition parties could take down the 
government or slippage surprises resurface despite a passed budget, tightening conditions in 
the same way. In this context, GDP would stall at +0.0% in 2027 and spread s reach 150 -
175bps. The ECB would talk up and potentially launch repo intervention. In our view, it would 
take a substantial Eurozone -wide contraction (France -0.5% in 2027 ; Eurozone -0.2%; OAT 
spreads at 175 -200bps) for the ECB to tweak or stop QT, launch open market and repo 
intervention and stabilize funding markets (as OATs and BTPs make up 50% of euro repo 
collateral). 
• Looking ahead, the 2027 elections are the next test, and TPI is a late and uncertain 
backstop. With the presidential election on 18 A pril and 2 May 2027 (legislative elections 
likely to follow), we see four scenarios: i) a President with an absolute majority and allies, who 
delivers fiscal consolidation; ii) a President short of a majority who seeks alliances  (our 
baseline, with the 2028 deficit at -4.9% and spreads above 100bps) ; iii) a President short of a 
majority without alliances  and iv) a political and fiscal crisis, for instance from unfunded tax 
cuts or spending hikes, with spreads above 200bps and widespread contagion. Only in t his 
last scenario would the ECB activate the Transmission Protection Instrument (TPI). The risk is 
a lack of political will rather than of instruments, and redenomination risk is the tail case.

Allianz Research | 9 October 2026 
Toasted, not burnt: France’s bond sell-off  
and the road to the 2027 election

### Page 2

France is the country most affected in the global bond sell-off

Since the start of the Hormuz crisis,  yields on 10 -year French government bonds (OAT) have risen by 150bps  
(vs 115bps for the US, 132bps for Italy and 83bps for Germany), the highest jump among developed economies. 
The feedback loop between rising oil prices and interest rate expectations  has met structurally vulnerable public 
finances whose credibility has been increasingly undermined by growing political fragility since the 2024 dissolution 
of the National Assembly. France’s debt-to-GDP ratio has surged from 98% in 2019 to over 119% in Q2 2026, with 
over 121% of GDP expected in 2027 (vs 83% to 87% in the Eurozone). Meanwhile, the  weakening economy (+0.5% 
GDP growth expected this year)  is leading to a fourth consecutive  year of fiscal slippage : The government now 
expects a deficit of -5.4% of GDP against the -5.1% targeted in the 2026 Budget. With Moody's upcoming review on 
23 October, France is now at risk of losing its AA rating (currently Aa3 by Moody’s vs A+ by S&P and Fitch Ratings, 
i.e. already one notch below); current pricing, if sustained, would even imply a BBB rating.

Table 1: Macroeconomic and fiscal forecasts for France in 2027

Government Allianz Research 
Real GDP growth (%) 1.0 0.9 
Inflation (CPI. %) 1.8 2.2 
Public balance (% GDP) -5.0 -5.3 
Primary balance (% GDP) -2.1 -2.5 
Debt service costs (% GDP) 2.9 2.8 
Public debt (% GDP) 121.7 121.8

Sources: French government, Allianz Research

France is the first victim of the Japanese repatriation trade.  More than other European sovereigns, France's 
investor base is exposed to price -sensitive players. The most volatile group, foreign banks and non -banks, holds 
36% of French government bonds. Domestic banks and non-banks, whose holdings fluctuate less, account for 32%, 
while non -price sensitive investors (domestic and foreign central banks) make up only 32%. Other European 
countries have either a stronger anchor in the domestic market (Italy  with 66% vs 47% for France ) or with foreign 
central banks ( Germany with 26% vs 16 % for France as Bunds as the primary FX reserve vehicle). Among foreign 
holders, Japanese investors play a significant role , holding around 5% of outstanding French government bonds 
(~EUR137bn). As interest rates rise in Japan, these positions become less attractive relative to  domestic holdings. 
At best they are no longer rolled over, at worst they are actively liquidated. In the recent bond sell -off France 
became the first victim of the J apanese repatriation trade, even though it plays only a minor role compared with 
the US as an overweight in Japanese investors' benchmark allocations .

Figure 1: France’s risk premium is very sensitive to changes in political fragility

(higher = more fragile), built from smoothed (Kalman filtered) poll-of-polls vote shares. Find more in our publication here. 
Sources: LSEG Datastream, Allianz Research
